**CmdRunAction (source=EventSource.AGENT, is_input=False)**
THOUGHT: 数据结构完全清晰：495人含信安46人（国家网安基地实验班），导师表98人315条安排，宿舍6楼栋。现在写完整解析脚本，做姓名匹配关联。
COMMAND:
mkdir -p /workspace/app-a7lm1eejtypt/tasks/parse && cd /workspace/app-a7lm1eejtypt/tasks && cat > parse/parse_all.py << 'EOF'
import pandas as pd, json, re

# ========== 1. 信安1班完整档案（46人） ==========
xa = pd.read_excel('信息安全1班学生人员基本情况.xlsx', header=0)
xa.columns = ['name','student_no','id_no','phone','political','hometown','gender','birth'][:len(xa.columns)]
xa = xa.dropna(subset=['name'])
profiles = []
for _, r in xa.iterrows():
    name = str(r['name']).strip()
    if not name or name == 'nan': continue
    idno = re.sub(r'\s','',str(r['id_no'] or ''))
    phone = re.sub(r'\s','',str(r['phone'] or ''))
    # 脱敏存储
    id_masked = (idno[:6]+'********'+idno[-4:]) if len(idno)>=10 else idno
    ph_masked = (phone[:3]+'****'+phone[-4:]) if len(phone)>=7 else phone
    ht = str(r['hometown'] or '').strip()
    prov = re.match(r'^(.{2,3}?(?:省|市|自治区))', ht)
    city_m = re.match(r'^(?:.{2,3}?(?:省|自治区))?(.{2,6}?(?:市|地区|州))', ht)
    profiles.append({
        'name': name, 'student_no': str(r['student_no']).strip(),
        'gender': str(r['gender']).strip(), 'birth': str(r['birth'])[:10],
        'political': str(r['political']).strip(),
        'hometown': ht, 'province': prov.group(1) if prov else ht[:3],
        'city': city_m.group(1) if city_m else '',
        'id_masked': id_masked, 'phone_masked': ph_masked,
    })
print('信安档案人数:', len(profiles))

# ========== 2. 495人全名单 ==========
n495 = pd.read_excel('计算机与大数据学院2024级新生495人名单及分班情况.xlsx', header=0)
roster = []
for _, r in n495.iterrows():
    roster.append({'student_no': str(r['学号']).strip(), 'major': str(r['专业']).strip(),
        'class_name': str(r['班级']).strip(), 'name': str(r['姓名']).strip(), 'gender': str(r['性别']).strip()})
print('495名单:', len(roster))

# ========== 3. 导师安排 315条 + 学生映射 ==========
mt = pd.read_excel('发学生-2025-2026学年计算机学院本科生导师指导工作安排一览表（22-25级）.xlsx', header=1)
mt.columns = ['mentor','title','target_class','count','names','qq'][:len(mt.columns)]
arrangements, mstudents = [], []
for _, r in mt.iterrows():
    mentor = str(r['mentor']).strip()
    if not mentor or mentor=='nan': continue
    names_raw = str(r['names'] or '').strip()
    name_list = [n.strip() for n in re.split(r'[、,，;；/]', names_raw) if n.strip() and n.strip()!='nan']
    arrangements.append({'mentor_name': mentor, 'title': str(r['title']).strip(),
        'target_class': str(r['target_class']).strip(), 'student_count': int(r['count']) if pd.notna(r['count']) else len(name_list),
        'qq': str(r['qq']).strip() if pd.notna(r['qq']) else '', 'student_names': name_list})
    for nm in name_list: mstudents.append({'mentor_name': mentor, 'title': str(r['title']).strip(), 'target_class': str(r['target_class']).strip(), 'student_name': nm})
print('导师安排条数:', len(arrangements), '导师-学生映射:', len(mstudents))

# ========== 4. 宿舍 6楼栋 ==========
sheets = ['毓英社区1号楼','毓英社区2号楼','立德社区5号楼','行合社区1号楼','行合社区6号楼','求实社区1号楼']
dorms = []
for sh in sheets:
    d = pd.read_excel('【20240828】计算机与大数据学院宿舍安排表.xlsx', sheet_name=sh, header=2)
    d = d.dropna(how='all')
    for _, r in d.iterrows():
        room = r.iloc[3]
        if pd.isna(room): continue
        room_no = re.sub(r'\.0$','',str(room).strip())
        if not re.match(r'^\d{3,4}$', room_no): continue
        building = sh; grade = re.sub(r'\.0$','',str(r.iloc[4]).strip()) if pd.notna(r.iloc[4]) else ''
        class_info = re.sub(r'\.0$','',str(r.iloc[5]).strip()) if pd.notna(r.iloc[5]) else ''
        years = re.sub(r'\.0$','',str(r.iloc[6]).strip()) if pd.notna(r.iloc[6]) else ''
        leader = str(r.iloc[8]).strip() if pd.notna(r.iloc[8]) else ''
        counselor = str(r.iloc[15]).strip() if pd.notna(r.iloc[15]) else ''
        for i in range(9, 15):  # 6个床位
            nm = r.iloc[i]
            if pd.isna(nm): continue
            nm = str(nm).strip()
            if not nm or nm=='nan': continue
            dorms.append({'building': building, 'room_no': room_no, 'seat_no': str(i-8),
                'grade': grade, 'class_info': class_info, 'years': years,
                'member_name': nm, 'is_leader': nm==leader, 'leader': leader, 'counselor': counselor})
print('宿舍床位记录:', len(dorms))

# ========== 5. 匹配信安46人 ==========
xa_names = {p['name'] for p in profiles}
mentor_map = {ms['student_name']: ms for ms in mstudents if ms['student_name'] in xa_names}
dorm_map = {dm['member_name']: dm for dm in dorms if dm['member_name'] in xa_names}
print('信安46人 导师匹配:', len(mentor_map), '宿舍匹配:', len(dorm_map))
# 495名单学号匹配
roster_map = {r['name']: r for r in roster if r['name'] in xa_names}
print('495名单匹配:', len(roster_map))

json.dump({'profiles': profiles, 'roster': roster, 'arrangements': arrangements,
           'mstudents': mstudents, 'dorms': dorms},
          open('parse/parsed.json','w',ensure_ascii=False), ensure_ascii=False)
print('SAVED parsed.json')
EOF
python3 parse/parse_all.py

COMMAND:
cd /workspace/app-a7lm1eejtypt/tasks && cat > parse/gen_sql.py << 'EOF'
import json
d = json.load(open('parse/parsed.json'))
profiles, roster, arrangements, mstudents, dorms = d['profiles'], d['roster'], d['arrangements'], d['mstudents'], d['dorms']

xa_names = {p['name'] for p in profiles}
mentor_map = {ms['student_name']: ms for ms in mstudents if ms['student_name'] in xa_names}
dorm_map = {dm['member_name']: dm for dm in dorms if dm['member_name'] in xa_names}
roster_map = {r['name']: r for r in roster if r['name'] in xa_names}

def esc(s): return str(s).replace("'", "''")
def arr_sql(names): return 'ARRAY[' + ','.join("'" + esc(n) + "'" for n in names) + ']::TEXT[]'

# 1. student_profiles（47份档案，关联导师/宿舍/495名单）
sqls = []
for p in profiles:
    nm = p['name']
    mt = mentor_map.get(nm); dm = dorm_map.get(nm); rs = roster_map.get(nm)
    vals = [p['student_no'], nm, p['gender'], p['birth'], p['political'], p['hometown'],
            p['province'], p['city'], p['id_masked'], p['phone_masked'],
            rs['major'] if rs else '', rs['class_name'] if rs else '信息安全1班',
            mt['mentor_name'] if mt else '', mt['title'] if mt else '', mt['qq'] if mt else '',
            dm['building'] if dm else '', dm['room_no'] if dm else '', dm['seat_no'] if dm else '',
            dm['counselor'] if dm else '', dm['class_info'] if dm else '']
    v = ",".join("'" + esc(x) + "'" for x in vals)
    sqls.append(f"INSERT INTO student_profiles (student_no,name,gender,birth_date,political_status,hometown,province,city,id_masked,phone_masked,major,class_name,mentor_name,mentor_title,mentor_qq,dorm_building,dorm_room,dorm_seat,dorm_counselor,dorm_class_info) VALUES ({v}) ON CONFLICT (student_no) DO NOTHING;")
open('parse/sql_profiles.sql','w').write('\n'.join(sqls))

# 2. mentor_arrangements 314条
sqls = []
for a in arrangements:
    v = f"'{esc(a['mentor_name'])}','{esc(a['title'])}','{esc(a['target_class'])}',{a['student_count']},{arr_sql(a['student_names'])},'{esc(a['qq'])}'"
    sqls.append(f"INSERT INTO mentor_arrangements (mentor_name,title,target_class,student_count,student_names,qq) VALUES ({v});")
open('parse/sql_mentors.sql','w').write('\n'.join(sqls))

# 3. dorm_members 2334条，分批
sqls = []
for dm in dorms:
    v = f"'{esc(dm['building'])}','{esc(dm['room_no'])}','{esc(dm['seat_no'])}','{esc(dm['grade'])}','{esc(dm['class_info'])}','{esc(dm['years'])}','{esc(dm['member_name'])}',{'true' if dm['is_leader'] else 'false'},'{esc(dm['leader'])}','{esc(dm['counselor'])}'"
    sqls.append(f"INSERT INTO dorm_members (building,room_no,seat_no,grade,class_info,years,member_name,is_leader,leader,counselor) VALUES ({v});")
for i in range(0, len(sqls), 600):
    open(f'parse/sql_dorms_{i//600}.sql','w').write('\n'.join(sqls[i:i+600]))

# 4. class_students 补第47人 + 全班刷新关联信息
sqls = ["INSERT INTO class_students (class_id, student_no, display_name) SELECT id, '" + p['student_no'][-4:] + "', '" + esc(p['name']) + "' FROM classes WHERE name='信息安全1班' ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;" if False else
        f"INSERT INTO class_students (class_id, student_no, display_name) SELECT c.id, '{p['student_no'][-4:]}', '{esc(p['name'])}' FROM classes c WHERE c.name = '信息安全1班' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM class_students cs WHERE cs.class_id = c.id AND cs.student_no = '{p['student_no'][-4:]}');"
        for p in profiles]
open('parse/sql_class_sync.sql','w').write('\n'.join(sqls))
print('SQL files generated')
for f in ['sql_profiles.sql','sql_mentors.sql','sql_class_sync.sql']:
    n = len(open(f'parse/{f}').read().strip().split(chr(10)))
    print(f, n, 'statements')
EOF
python3 parse/gen_sql.py && ls parse/*.sql | xargs wc -l

**CmdRunAction (source=EventSource.AGENT, is_input=False)**
THOUGHT: 无本地连接。将SQL压缩为多行INSERT格式减小体积，然后整批传入：
COMMAND:
cd /workspace/app-a7lm1eejtypt/tasks/parse && python3 << 'EOF'
import json, re
d = json.load(open('parsed.json'))
profiles, roster, arrangements, mstudents, dorms = d['profiles'], d['roster'], d['arrangements'], d['mstudents'], d['dorms']
xa_names = {p['name'] for p in profiles}
mentor_map = {ms['student_name']: ms for ms in mstudents if ms['student_name'] in xa_names}
qq_map = {a['mentor_name']: a['qq'] for a in arrangements if a.get('qq')}
dorm_map = {dm['member_name']: dm for dm in dorms if dm['member_name'] in xa_names}
roster_map = {r['name']: r for r in roster if r['name'] in xa_names}
def esc(s): return str(s).replace("'","''")

# profiles 多行INSERT
rows = []
for p in profiles:
    nm = p['name']; mt = mentor_map.get(nm); dm = dorm_map.get(nm); rs = roster_map.get(nm)
    vals = [p['student_no'],nm,p['gender'],p['birth'],p['political'],p['hometown'],p['province'],p['city'],p['id_masked'],p['phone_masked'],
            rs['major'] if rs else '',rs['class_name'] if rs else '信息安全1班',
            mt['mentor_name'] if mt else '',mt['title'] if mt else '',qq_map.get(mt['mentor_name'],'') if mt else '',
            dm['building'] if dm else '',dm['room_no'] if dm else '',dm['seat_no'] if dm else '',dm['counselor'] if dm else '',dm['class_info'] if dm else '']
    rows.append('(' + ','.join("'"+esc(x)+"'" for x in vals) + ')')
sql1 = "INSERT INTO student_profiles (student_no,name,gender,birth_date,political_status,hometown,province,city,id_masked,phone_masked,major,class_name,mentor_name,mentor_title,mentor_qq,dorm_building,dorm_room,dorm_seat,dorm_counselor,dorm_class_info) VALUES\n" + ',\n'.join(rows) + " ON CONFLICT (student_no) DO NOTHING;"
open('c_profiles.sql','w').write(sql1); print('profiles:', len(sql1))

# class_students 同步47人
rows = []
for p in profiles:
    s4 = p['student_no'][-4:]
    rows.append(f"SELECT c.id,'{s4}','{esc(p['name'])}' FROM classes c WHERE c.name='信息安全1班' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM class_students cs WHERE cs.class_id=c.id AND cs.student_no='{s4}')")
sql2 = "INSERT INTO class_students (class_id,student_no,display_name)\n" + '\nUNION ALL\n'.join(rows) + ';'
open('c_class.sql','w').write(sql2); print('class:', len(sql2))

# mentors 多行INSERT
rows = []
for a in arrangements:
    ns = 'ARRAY[' + ','.join("'"+esc(n)+"'" for n in a['student_names']) + ']'
    rows.append(f"('{esc(a['mentor_name'])}','{esc(a['title'])}','{esc(a['target_class'])}',{a['student_count']},{ns},'{esc(a['qq'])}')")
sql3 = "INSERT INTO mentor_arrangements (mentor_name,title,target_class,student_count,student_names,qq) VALUES\n" + ',\n'.join(rows) + ';'
open('c_mentors.sql','w').write(sql3); print('mentors:', len(sql3))

# dorms 多行INSERT 分4批
rows = []
for dm in dorms:
    rows.append(f"('{esc(dm['building'])}','{esc(dm['room_no'])}','{esc(dm['seat_no'])}','{esc(dm['grade'])}','{esc(dm['class_info'])}','{esc(dm['years'])}','{esc(dm['member_name'])}',{'true' if dm['is_leader'] else 'false'},'{esc(dm['leader'])}','{esc(dm['counselor'])}')")
hdr = "INSERT INTO dorm_members (building,room_no,seat_no,grade,class_info,years,member_name,is_leader,leader,counselor) VALUES\n"
batch = 600
for i in range(0, len(rows), batch):
    open(f'c_dorms_{i//batch}.sql','w').write(hdr + ',\n'.join(rows[i:i+batch]) + ';')
    print(f'dorms batch {i//batch}:', len(hdr + ',\n'.join(rows[i:i+batch])))
EOF
</action>

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Target, Clock, Users, PlayCircle, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { getTrainingTasks } from '@/db/training';
import { SCENE_OPTIONS, type TrainingTask } from '@/types/training';

export default function TrainingTaskListPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<TrainingTask[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // 学生端展示所有已发布任务（试点班级内）
    const load = async () => {
      const list = await getTrainingTasks('');
      setTasks(list);
    };
    load();
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="container py-8 max-w-4xl">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">实训任务</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-2">完成教师下发的软素养对话式对抗实训任务，系统将完整留存你的对话交互证据链。</p>
      </div>

      {tasks.length === 0 ? (
        <Card className="border-dashed">
          <CardContent className="py-16 text-center text-muted-foreground">
            <Target className="h-12 w-12 mx-auto mb-4 opacity-20" />
            <p>暂无实训任务</p>
            <p className="text-sm mt-1">等待教师下发任务后即可参加</p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {tasks.map((t) => {
            const scene = SCENE_OPTIONS.find((s) => s.value === t.scene_type);
            return (
              <Card key={t.id} className="hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                <CardContent className="p-5">
                  <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-4">
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                        <h3 className="font-semibold text-base truncate">{t.title}</h3>
                        <Badge variant="outline" className="shrink-0">{scene?.label || t.scene_type}</Badge>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground line-clamp-2">{t.description}</p>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Target className="h-3 w-3" />难度：{t.difficulty}</span>
                        {t.deadline && <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Clock className="h-3 w-3" />截止：{new Date(t.deadline).toLocaleString('zh-CN')}</span>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <Button onClick={() => navigate(`/student/training/${t.id}`)} className="shrink-0">
                      <PlayCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" /> 进入实训
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// 实训业务类型定义（对应 training_practice 业务表）

export interface ClassInfo {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  university_email: string;
  description?: string;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface ClassStudent {
  id: string;
  class_id: string;
  student_no: string;
  gender?: string;
  political_status?: string;
  display_name: string;
  created_at: string;
}

// 实训场景类型
export type SceneType =
  | 'project_defense'   // 项目结题答辩
  | 'tech_report'       // 技术方案汇报
  | 'conflict_communication' // 小组冲突沟通
  | 'pressure_query';   // 压力质询

export interface TrainingTask {
  id: string;
  title: string;
    })
    .select()
    .maybeSingle();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;

// ==================== 班级学生 ====================

export async function getClassStudents(classId: string): Promise<ClassStudent[]> {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('class_students')
    .select('*')
    .eq('class_id', classId)
    .order('student_no', { ascending: true })
    .limit(500);
  return Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
}

export async function importClassStudents(classId: string, students: { student_no: string; gender?: string; political_status?: string; display_name: string }[]): Promise<number> {
  const rows = students.map((s) => ({
    class_id: classId,
    student_no: s.student_no,
    gender: s.gender || null,
    political_status: s.political_status || null,
    display_name: s.display_name,
  }));
  const { error } = await supabase.from('class_students').upsert(rows, { onConflict: 'class_id,student_no' });
  if (error) throw error;
  return rows.length;
}

```
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
```

```
// 学生真实身份（从班级名单选择，存完整学号）
function getStudentIdentity(): string {
  return localStorage.getItem('student_no') || '';
}

export default function TrainingTaskListPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<TrainingTask[]>([]);
  const [students, setStudents] = useState<ClassStudent[]>([]);
  const [studentNo, setStudentNo] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const load = async () => {
      // 加载真实班级名单（信息安全1班）
      const classes = await getClasses('demo@university.com');
      const cls = classes.find((c) => c.name === '信息安全1班') || classes[0];
      if (cls) {
        const list = await getClassStudents(cls.id);
        setStudents(list);
      }
      // 学生端展示所有已发布任务（试点班级内）
      const tl = await getTrainingTasks('');
      setTasks(tl);
    };
    load();
    setStudentNo(getStudentIdentity());
  }, []);

  // 查询当前学生已完成的提交
  const [doneSet, setDoneSet] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  useEffect(() => {
    const loadDone = async () => {
      if (!studentNo) return;
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('training_submissions')
        .select('task_id, status')
        .eq('student_no', studentNo);
      const set = new Set<string>();
      (data as { task_id: string; status: string }[] || []).forEach((r) => {
        if (r.status === 'completed') set.add(r.task_id);
      });
      setDoneSet(set);
    };
    loadDone();
  }, [studentNo]);

  const handleSelectStudent = (v: string) => {
    setStudentNo(v);
    localStorage.setItem('student_no', v);
    toast.success('身份已切换');
  };

  return (
    <div className="container py-8 max-w-4xl">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 mb-8">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">实训任务</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-2 text-pretty">完成教师下发的软素养对话式对抗实训任务，系统将完整留存你的对话交互证据链。</p>
        </div>
        <Select value={studentNo} onValueChange={handleSelectStudent}>
          <SelectTrigger className="w-44 shrink-0"><SelectValue placeholder="选择学号" /></SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent>
            {students.map((s) => (
              <SelectItem key={s.student_no} value={s.student_no}>{s.student_no} · {s.display_name}</SelectItem>
            ))}
          </SelectContent>
        </Select>
      </div>

      {!studentNo && (
        <Card className="mb-6 border-primary/30 bg-primary/5">
          <CardContent className="flex items-center gap-3 py-4">
            <UserCircle className="h-5 w-5 text-primary shrink-0" />
            <p className="text-sm">请先在右上角选择你的学号，完成实训后成绩将计入你的档案。</p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}
```

b1 = hdr + ',\n'.join(rows[:half]) + ';'
b2 = hdr + ',\n'.join(rows[half:]) + ';'
open('m_b1.txt','w').write(b1)
open('m_b2.txt','w').write(b2)
print('b1:', len(b1), 'b2:', len(b2))

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ChevronLeft, Users, TrendingDown, BookOpen, AlertTriangle, Award, Target } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select';
import { Radar, RadarChart, PolarGrid, PolarAngleAxis, PolarRadiusAxis, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip } from 'recharts';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';
import { RADAR_DIMENSIONS, type ClassInfo, type TrainingSubmission } from '@/types/training';

// 生效分数：教师复核覆盖优先
function effectiveScores(s: TrainingSubmission): Record<string, number> {
  const base: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const d of RADAR_DIMENSIONS) {
    const ov = s.teacher_override?.[d.key];
    base[d.key] = typeof ov === 'number' ? ov : Number(s.scores?.[d.key] ?? 0);
  }
  return base;
}

export default function ClassPortraitPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const universityEmail = user?.email || localStorage.getItem('university_email') || '';

  const [classes, setClasses] = useState<ClassInfo[]>([]);
  const [selectedClass, setSelectedClass] = useState('');
  const [submissions, setSubmissions] = useState<TrainingSubmission[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadClasses = async () => {
      if (!universityEmail) return;
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('classes')
        .select('*')
        .eq('university_email', universityEmail)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(100);
      const list = (data as ClassInfo[]) || [];
      setClasses(list);
      if (list.length > 0) setSelectedClass(list[0].id);
    };
    loadClasses();
  }, [universityEmail]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadData = async () => {
      if (!selectedClass) return;
      setLoading(true);
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('training_submissions')
        .select('*')
        .eq('class_id', selectedClass)
        .eq('status', 'completed')
        .order('updated_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(500);

import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts";
import { corsHeaders } from "../_shared/cors.ts";
import { withMonitor } from "../_shared/logger.ts";

const BAIDU_API_KEY = Deno.env.get('BAIDU_API_KEY') || "";
const BAIDU_SECRET_KEY = Deno.env.get('BAIDU_SECRET_KEY') || "";

async function getAccessToken() {
  const url = `https://aip.baidubce.com/oauth/2.0/token?grant_type=client_credentials&client_id=${BAIDU_API_KEY}&client_secret=${BAIDU_SECRET_KEY}`;
  const response = await fetch(url, { method: "POST" });
  const data = await response.json();
  return data.access_token;
}

async function callBaiduLLM(messages: any[]) {
  const token = await getAccessToken();
  const url = `https://aip.baidubce.com/rpc/2.0/ai_custom/v1/wenxinworkshop/chat/completions_pro?access_token=${token}`;
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ messages }),
  });
  const data = await response.json();
  return data.result || "";
}

New String: ```
const BAIDU_API_KEY = Deno.env.get('BAIDU_API_KEY') || "";
const BAIDU_SECRET_KEY = Deno.env.get('BAIDU_SECRET_KEY') || "";

// 查询学生真实档案（导师/宿舍/生源地/政治面貌），用于画像与脚本个性化
async function fetchStudentBackground(studentNo: string): Promise<string> {
  if (!studentNo) return "";
  try {
    const url = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') || "";
    const key = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') || "";
    if (!url || !key) return "";
    const res = await fetch(`${url}/rest/v1/student_profiles?student_no=eq.${encodeURIComponent(studentNo)}&select=*`, {
      headers: { apikey: key, Authorization: `Bearer ${key}` },
    });
    const rows = await res.json();
    const p = Array.isArray(rows) && rows[0];
    if (!p) return "";
    const parts = [
      p.name ? `姓名：${p.name}` : "",
      p.major ? `专业：${p.major}` : "",
      p.class_name ? `班级：${p.class_name}` : "",
      p.gender ? `性别：${p.gender}` : "",
      p.political_status ? `政治面貌：${p.political_status}` : "",
      p.hometown ? `生源地：${p.hometown}` : "",
      p.mentor_name ? `指导导师：${p.mentor_name}（${p.mentor_title || ''}）` : "",
      p.dorm_building && p.dorm_room ? `宿舍：${p.dorm_building} ${p.dorm_room}室` : "",
      p.dorm_room && p.dorm_seat ? `床位：${p.dorm_seat}号` : "",
    ].filter(Boolean);
    return parts.join("\n");
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("fetchStudentBackground error", e);
    return "";
  }
}
```

  const completed = submissions;

  // 班级各维度平均分
  const radarData = useMemo(() => {
    return RADAR_DIMENSIONS.map((d) => {
      const vals = completed.map((s) => effectiveScores(s)[d.key]).filter((v) => v > 0);
      const avg = vals.length > 0 ? Math.round(vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / vals.length) : 0;
      return { dimension: d.label, score: avg };
    });
  }, [completed]);

  const weakestDim = useMemo(() => {
    if (radarData.length === 0) return null;
    return [...radarData].sort((a, b) => a.score - b.score)[0];
  }, [radarData]);

    if (radarData.length === 0) return null;

        const eff = effectiveScores(s);

          .sort((a, b) => a.score - b.score)[0];

      })
      .sort((a, b) => a.avg - b.avg);

          </p>

"""  const weakStudents = useMemo(() => {
    const profileMap = new Map(profiles.map((p) => [p.student_no, p]));
    return completed
      .map((s) => {
        const eff = effectiveScores(s);
        const avg = Math.round(RADAR_DIMENSIONS.reduce((a, d) => a + eff[d.key], 0) / RADAR_DIMENSIONS.length);
        const weakest = RADAR_DIMENSIONS.map((d) => ({ label: d.label, score: eff[d.key] }))

        // 完整学号直接匹配；兼容历史后4位提交
        const prof = profileMap.get(s.student_no) || profiles.find((p) => p.student_no.endsWith(s.student_no));
        return { student_no: s.student_no, avg, weakest, prof };
      })
      .sort((a, b) => a.avg - b.avg);
  }, [completed, profiles]);

  const [groupBy, setGroupBy] = useState('none');
  const groupedStudents = useMemo(() => {
    if (groupBy === 'none') return null;
    const map = new Map<string, typeof weakStudents>();
    for (const s of weakStudents) {
      const key = groupBy === 'mentor' ? (s.prof?.mentor_name || '未分配导师') : (s.prof?.dorm_building ? `${s.prof.dorm_building}${s.prof.dorm_room}室` : '未安排宿舍');
      if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, []);
      map.get(key)!.push(s);
    }
    return Array.from(map.entries()).sort((a, b) => b[1].length - a[1].length);
  }, [groupBy, weakStudents]);""")

// ==================== 班级 ====================

export async function getClasses(email: string): Promise<ClassInfo[]> {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('classes')
    .select('*')
    .eq('university_email', email)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(100);
  return Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
}
